### [74] [SASGeo: Stability-Aware Semantic Map Localization for GNSS-Denied UAVs -- A Framework and Synthetic Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07737)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SAS语义地图定位框架，利用道路、建筑等持久性结构特征实现GNSS缺失环境下的无人机绝对位置修正。在220次随机检索试验中，空间语义匹配变体达到94.5-95.5%的Recall@1，优于全局语义描述符的58.6%，证明结构化语义几何能有效区分受控跨视角扰动下的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS缺失无人机需要绝对位置修正来约束视觉-惯性里程计的漂移，而现有跨视角图像检索方法对季节、光照、视角、地图时效和传感器模态变化敏感，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SAS语义地图定位框架，结合语义栅格对齐、关系图证据、特征稳定性和地理独特性、显式的正/矛盾/未知观测分类，以及完整性感知的模糊定位拒绝机制，并给出了具体的加权和决策模型。

Result: 220次随机检索试验中，全局语义描述符Recall@1为58.6%，空间语义匹配变体达94.5-95.5%。Wilson 95%置信区间显示全局描述符与空间变体之间存在显著差异，但各空间变体间区间重叠。

Conclusion: 结构化语义几何能在受控跨视角扰动下有效区分位置，但实验尚未验证实际飞行导航，后续需解决更困难的位置混淆、地图时效性问题和定位拒绝测试。

Abstract: GNSS-denied unmanned aerial vehicles require occasional absolute position fixes to bound the drift of visual-inertial odometry. Cross-view image retrieval can provide such fixes, but raw appearance is sensitive to season, illumination, viewpoint, map age, and sensor modality. We propose \sas, a semantic map-localization framework that represents the environment through persistent structures such as roads, buildings, waterways, railways, intersections, and field boundaries. The method combines semantic raster alignment, relational graph evidence, feature stability and geographic distinctiveness, explicit positive/contradictory/unknown observations, and integrity-aware rejection of ambiguous fixes. Unlike a broad architecture-only proposal, this paper specifies concrete weighting and decision models and reports a reproducible synthetic proof of concept. In 220 randomized retrieval trials with rotation, scale changes, partial crops, occlusion, simulated map changes, and hard semantic decoys, a global semantic descriptor achieved 58.6\% Recall@1, while spatial semantic matching variants achieved 94.5-95.5%. Wilson 95\% intervals separate the global descriptor from the spatial variants but overlap among the spatial variants, so the experiment supports semantic geometry rather than a definitive benefit from each proposed module. The preliminary experiment does not validate real-flight navigation; rather, it demonstrates that structured semantic geometry can discriminate locations under controlled cross-view perturbations and identifies the harder aliasing, map-aging, and rejection tests required next.

</details>


### [75] [Physics-Guided Biomechanical Gait Adaptation for Humanoid Locomotion on Extreme Sloped Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07830)
*Xuanyu Chen,Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Zhihao Gu,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Haiyue Zhu,Shijun Yan,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HumoSlope两阶段框架，通过斜坡自适应ZMP正则化（Stage I）和生物力学斜坡步态适配器BSGA（Stage II），实现了盲人形机器人在高达62.7%（32.1°）户外草地斜坡上的鲁棒连续行走，有效避免了低重心蹲伏步态退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习在平地/离散地形上已取得显著成果，但在陡坡上因持续重力偏差需要同时兼顾稳定性和姿态控制，通用奖励设计易导致策略收敛到保守的低重心蹲伏步态，目前斜坡地形人形机器人控制尚缺乏有效解决方案。

Method: 两阶段物理引导框架：（1）Stage I引入斜坡自适应ZMP正则化器，在局部倾斜支撑面而非世界水平参考面上评估ZMP，建立地形一致的平衡先验；（2）Stage II提出生物力学斜坡步态适配器BSGA，以宏观地形描述符作为特权信号（仅训练时使用），动态门控软奖励先验，根据估计的斜坡几何调节CoM高度和下肢协调——鼓励髋主导的上坡推进和膝主导的下坡制动，部署时仅依赖本体感觉。

Result: Sim-to-Real实验表明，该方法有效缓解了姿态退化问题，实现了盲人形机器人在户外草地斜坡上连续行走，最大坡度达62.7%（32.1°）。

Conclusion: 验证了物理引导方法在人形机器人挑战性斜坡地形适应中的有效性，通过将地形几何知识注入训练过程，在无需在线外感受感知的条件下实现了鲁棒的斜坡行走。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning has enabled impressive humanoid locomotion; however, control on steep slopes remains largely unexplored. Unlike flat or discrete terrains, sloped terrains impose a persistent gravitational bias that demands simultaneous stability and posture control. Consequently, under generic reward formulations, policies can converge to slow, conservative low-center-of-mass (CoM) crouched gaits.
  In this work, we propose a novel two-stage physics-guided framework, dubbed HumoSlope, dedicated to robust humanoid locomotion on diverse sloped terrains. Specifically, Stage I establishes a terrain-consistent balance prior by introducing a slope-adaptive Zero Moment Point (ZMP) regularizer evaluated directly on the local inclined support plane rather than a world-horizontal reference. To prevent the resulting policy from defaulting to a crouched posture, Stage II introduces the Biomechanical Slope Gait Adapter (BSGA). Utilizing extracted macroscopic terrain descriptors as privileged, training-only signals, BSGA dynamically gates soft reward priors to modulate CoM height and lower-limb coordination based on the estimated slope geometry -- encouraging hip-dominant uphill propulsion and knee-oriented downhill braking. Crucially, the deployed actor remains entirely proprioceptive, requiring no online exteroceptive sensing.
  Extensive Sim-to-Real experiments demonstrate that our framework effectively mitigates posture degeneration and enables blind, continuous traversal of outdoor grass slopes up to 62.7% ($32.1^\circ$), validating a physics-guided approach to challenging slope terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [76] [Shift & Drift: A Zero-Shot Benchmark for Generalizable and Robust Autonomous Driving Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07844)
*Alessandro Canevaro,Hang Yu,Julian Schmidt,Peizheng Li,Silvan Lindner,Wilhelm Stork,Georg Martius,Julian Jordan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Shift & Drift双轨基准测试，分别评估运动规划器在语义分布偏移（利用航拍数据转换到nuPlan框架，测试跨城市泛化）和状态分布偏移（注入随机扰动，测试执行误差鲁棒性）下的表现。实验发现模仿学习方法在分布内表现好但泛化差，强化学习方法退化更平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关环运动规划器在大规模数据集上表现优异，但在陌生城市拓扑结构下的泛化能力以及执行扰动后的恢复机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建双轨基准：语义偏移轨将DeepScenario Open 3D航拍数据转换为nuPlan仿真框架，实现零样本跨城市评估；状态分布漂移轨向自车动力学注入随机扰动以量化鲁棒性。系统评估多种规划范式在两种偏移下的失效模式。

Result: 模仿学习方法在分布内基准得分高，但在语义偏移下（尤其行人密集环境）显著失效，且受时间相关执行噪声时持续漂移；强化学习方法退化更平滑，安全性和进度指标更优。

Conclusion: 发现模仿保真度与闭环鲁棒性之间存在经验性权衡，为社区提供了评估可靠部署进展的严格基准。

Abstract: While closed-loop motion planners trained on large-scale, object-level datasets, e.g., nuPlan, demonstrate strong in-distribution (ID) performance, their generalization to novel urban topologies and recovery mechanisms following execution perturbations remain under-explored. To address this, we present Shift & Drift, a novel dual-track benchmark designed to rigorously stress-test motion planners across two critical axes of distribution shift: (1) The Semantic Shift Track leverages a novel conversion pipeline that transforms the aerial, DeepScenario Open 3D dataset into the nuPlan simulation framework. This enables zero-shot evaluation of planners trained on North American and Singaporean data against 1,182 scenarios spanning four German cities and the US city of San Francisco featuring dense pedestrian-cyclist interactions. (2) The State-Distribution Drift Track injects stochastic perturbations into the ego vehicle's dynamics to quantify robustness against compounding execution errors. Based on this, we systematically evaluate the failure modes of diverse planning paradigms under semantic and state-distribution shifts. While imitation learning methods achieve high scores in ID benchmarks, they exhibit significant failures under semantic shift, particularly in pedestrian-dense environments, and suffer from persistent drift when subjected to temporally correlated actuation noise. In contrast, the evaluated reinforcement-learning-based planner demonstrates more graceful degradation, maintaining higher safety and progress metrics across both tracks. Our findings reveal an empirical trade-off between imitation fidelity and closed-loop resilience, providing the community with a rigorous benchmark to evaluate progress toward reliable deployment.

</details>


### [77] [STEMbot: A Compliant Robot for Under-Canopy Plant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07873)
*Zachary Charlick,Nilay Roy Choudhury,Haoyu Ma,Xiaonan Huang,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEMbot是一种微型攀爬机器人系统，能够在植物冠层下自主导航，通过集成PIN-SLAM定位与语义八叉树建图、流形约束A*路径规划及光线追踪目标指定，实现了对叶背和茎干等隐蔽部位的早期害虫检测，在多种植物上验证了高精度自主导航能力（平均Chamfer距离<1cm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机农业的害虫监测受限于人工成本，现有无人机/地面机器人只能检测植物上方或侧面，而许多害虫生活在叶背或茎干上，发现时已造成显著损害，因此需要能够深入植物冠层内部进行早期检测的微型攀爬机器人。

Method: 1) 微型攀爬机器人硬件设计；2) 集成几何PIN-SLAM与语义八叉树实现鲁棒定位与建图；3) 流形约束A*路径规划实现分支感知遍历；4) 光线追踪目标指定实现遮挡目标检测。

Result: 在7-33mm茎干上实现可靠攀爬，在四种不同植物上完成自主导航，重建精度平均Chamfer距离小于1cm（与离线摄影测量基线对比），证明了全局一致的里程计精度。

Conclusion: STEMbot首次实现了具有机载感知能力的植物冠层内自主攀爬导航，可用于隐蔽害虫的早期检测，有望降低有机农业的劳动力成本。

Abstract: The scalability of organic agriculture is partially limited by the labor costs associated with monitoring for pests. While drones and rovers are well-suited for agricultural monitoring from above or next to plants, many pests live on the underside of leaves or on plant stems, making them detectable only after they have caused significant damage. To enable early pest detection we present STEMbot, a miniature climbing robot system designed for autonomous navigation under plant canopies. Unlike existing climbing platforms that lack on-board perception or are restricted to unbranched vertical trunks, STEMbot integrates a fully geometric PIN-SLAM pipeline with a semantic OcTree to achieve robust localization and mapping while climbing the plant. To plan STEMbot's motion we propose a manifold-constrained A* planner along with ray-tracing goal specification to enable branch-aware traversal and the inspection of occluded targets. We validate our system through hardware experiments, demonstrating reliable traversal of stems ranging from 7-33mm and autonomous navigation across four distinct plant specimens. Quantitative evaluations show that our system achieves high-fidelity geometric reconstructions with an average Chamfer distance of less than 1cm relative to an offline photogrammetry baseline, confirming that STEMbot maintains the globally consistent odometry needed for autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [78] [Time-to-Collision Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance Using Pretrained Vision Models for Robots in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07885)
*Erik Jagnandan,Mulugeta Haile,Gregory Barber,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效、可解释的视觉动态避障方法，完全基于真实数据运行，利用预训练的单目深度估计模型UniDepth和特征点跟踪管线（SuperPoint+SuperGlue），通过计算每个关键点的碰撞时间（TTC）并选择2D运动基元规避障碍，无需模型训练，仅需74秒数据调参，在M3ED数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化户外环境中动态障碍物规避仍是自主移动机器人的关键挑战，现有方法依赖大规模机器人专用训练数据或基于仿真的策略，存在sim-to-real迁移问题且数据效率低下。

Method: 1) 利用预训练UniDepth模型从RGB视频生成密集深度图；2) 采用SuperPoint+SuperGlue特征点跟踪管线进行长序列关键点跟踪；3) 利用相机内参和预测深度将2D像素投影至3D；4) 执行束调整优化3D关键点；5) 计算每个关键点的碰撞时间（TTC）；6) 选择2D地面运动基元使机器人远离最小TTC关键点的最近接近点。

Result: 在M3ED数据集上，识别TTC<1秒帧的精确率0.49、召回率0.38；真阳性检测中84%正确生成规避运动方向；22个独特物理障碍中检测到20个（20/22）的TTC<1秒帧；仅需74秒数据用于超参数调优。

Conclusion: 该方法完全消除模型训练需求，展现了极高的数据效率，同时在不同障碍类型上保持可解释和可泛化的行为，避免了sim-to-real迁移问题。

Abstract: Dynamic obstacle avoidance in unstructured outdoor environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous mobile robots, particularly when large-scale robot-specific training data and simulation-based policies are impractical. We present a data-efficient, interpretable method for vision-based dynamic obstacle avoidance that operates entirely on real-world data, avoiding the sim-to-real transfer problem inherent in simulation-trained policies. Our approach leverages UniDepth, a large pretrained monocular depth estimation model, to produce dense depth maps from RGB video without requiring stereo cameras or LiDAR at inference time. Dynamic obstacle avoidance is achieved by extending the SuperPoint and SuperGlue feature correspondence pipeline to track keypoints across long frame sequences, projecting their 2D pixel-space positions into 3D using camera intrinsics and predicted depth, running bundle adjustment initialized from these 3D keypoints, and computing per-keypoint time-to-collision (TTC). A 2D motion primitive in the ground plane is then selected to move the robot away from the closest point of approach of the minimum-TTC keypoint. Evaluated on real-world data from the M3ED dataset, our pipeline achieves a precision of 0.49 and a recall of 0.38 in identifying frames with a ground truth TTC below 1 second, and correctly generates the evasive motion direction in 84\% of true positive detections. Crucially, it detects at least one frame with TTC less than 1 second for 20 out of 22 unique physical obstacles present in our test sequences. Unlike end-to-end learned methods that demand thousands of hours of robot-specific training data, our approach eliminates model training entirely, requiring only 74 seconds of data for hyperparameter tuning. This demonstrates exceptional data efficiency while preserving interpretable and generalizable behavior across diverse obstacle types.

</details>


### [79] [Monocular Vision Based Control Framework for Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07897)
*Shail Jadav,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的单目视觉抓取框架，通过RGB输入和位置控制夹爪，结合开放词汇目标检测、分割、点追踪、单目深度估计和基于语言描述的刚度估计模型，实现对软质和刚性物体的稳定抓取，并在Franka Emika Research 3机械臂上以生菜、马苏里拉奶酪、可颂、纸巾和塑料瓶等物体验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取方法通常针对软质或刚性物体分别处理，依赖触觉感知、物体特定模型或专用夹爪，缺乏统一的、传感器高效的通用解决方案。

Method: 融合开放词汇目标检测、图像分割、边界感知点分配、实时点追踪和单目深度估计；核心是基于语言描述的刚度估计模型，利用语义信息推断物体柔顺性以预选抓取策略；软质物体通过Procrustes不相似度度量（基于追踪关键点）进行抓取适应，刚性物体则通过追踪点距离缩放调节夹爪宽度。

Result: 在真实拾放实验中，对生菜、马苏里拉奶酪、可颂、纸巾和硬塑料瓶等机械属性差异显著的物体，实现了稳定抓取。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了仅凭视觉反馈即可在单一控制框架下处理软硬物体，为食品处理和家庭操作提供了一种实用、传感器高效且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Grasping in unstructured environments requires handling objects with widely different mechanical properties, from soft and deformable items to rigid everyday objects. Most existing approaches address these categories separately and often rely on tactile sensing, object-specific models, or specialized grippers. In this paper, we present a unified monocular vision-based grasping framework that targets both soft and rigid objects within a single control pipeline, using only RGB input and a position-controlled gripper. The proposed system combines open-vocabulary object detection, image segmentation, boundary-aware point assignment, real-time point tracking, and monocular depth estimation to recover object motion and geometry from visual observations. A key component of the framework is a language-based stiffness estimation model that infers an object's expected compliance from its semantic description and provides an object-level prior for selecting the grasping strategy before contact. For deformable objects, grasp adaptation is governed by a Procrustes-based dissimilarity measure computed from tracked keypoints, which acts as a visual proxy for deformation. For rigid objects, the gripper width is regulated through the scaling of tracked point distances. We validate the proposed method in real-world pick-and-place experiments on a Franka Emika Research 3 arm using objects with substantially different mechanical properties, including lettuce, fresh mozzarella cheese, croissants, paper towels, and hard plastic bottles. Results demonstrate that the framework achieves stable grasping across both soft and rigid objects using visual feedback alone, highlighting a practical, sensor-efficient, and generalizable approach for food handling and household manipulation.

</details>


### [80] [Towards Soft Robotic Exogloves for Musculoskeletal Manipulation to Reduce Pain and Spasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07958)
*Antonia Salluce,Maeryn Erdheim,Gailen Davis,Lauren H. Sullivan,Max-William Kanz,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软体机器人外骨骼手套的初步开发方案，该手套既能辅助手部运动，又能通过按摩式压缩缓解痉挛肌肉疼痛，解决了现有方案无法同时应对疼痛和运动障碍的问题。研究包括个性化软体气动执行器的设计、有限元分析、优化、制造及初步实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约1200万人受手部痉挛及相关疼痛困扰（包括中风幸存者、关节炎患者等），现有软体机器人外骨骼手套仅能辅助运动或缓解疼痛之一，缺乏同时解决两个问题的方案。

Method: 设计了个性化的软体气动执行器，分别针对手背（优化贴合与力分布）、手掌（3D打印复杂几何）和手指腹侧（可压缩以适应痉挛屈曲手指）；使用有限元建模（100和200 kPa）进行应力分析与几何优化，最终组装成外骨骼手套并进行初步用户体验测试。

Result: 手背执行器成功定制化贴合参与者手部拓扑，实现完全共形接触和最大力分布；手指腹侧执行器成功制造并可大幅压缩以适应痉挛手指空间；手掌执行器通过立体光刻成功打印，展示了3D打印软体执行器实现复杂几何的潜力；手套成功组装并由试点用户验证了舒适性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该软体外骨骼手套在同时实现运动辅助和疼痛缓解方面具有初步可行性，个性化设计和3D打印技术为定制化康复设备提供了新思路，但尚处于早期开发阶段，需进一步研究验证。

Abstract: Hand spasticity and resulting pain affect 12 million people worldwide, including stroke survivors, arthritis patients, and those with other muscle and nerve deficiencies. Soft robotic exogloves are being introduced to help patients enhance mobility or manage pain; however, there are no current solutions that address both pain and mobility. We present preliminary development of a soft robotic exoglove that both aids in mobility and administers massage-like compression to relax spastic muscles. The glove consists of soft pneumatic actuators that are personalized to an individual's hand topology and kinematics, allowing for optimal conformability and targeted mobility. Novel soft actuators were designed, analyzed, fabricated, assembled into an exoglove, and experimentally tested. Actuators were 3D modeled and analyzed with finite element modeling under pressures of 100 and 200 kPa. Geometries were optimized to minimize stress before fabrication and testing. A dorsal finger actuator was successfully customized to a participant's hand topology, providing full conformal contact and maximal force distribution. A ventral finger actuator was successfully fabricated that can be drastically compressed in size to fit into the tight space of a hyperflexed spastic finger. A palmar actuator was successfully printed with stereolithography, showing potential for 3D-printed soft actuators with more complex geometries. The glove was assembled and successfully worn by a pilot user to validate initial findings in comfort and effectiveness.

</details>


### [81] [Soft Robotic Exogloves for Dexterous Mobility -- Towards Personalized Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07968)
*Paul Dela Cruz,Mostafa Mo. Massoud,Jacqueline Libby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑扫描和硅胶模具铸造的个性化气动软体机器人外手套，通过有限元分析和气动压力控制实验，验证了个性化设计在手部解剖适配、人机接触力分析及关节精准运动方面的有效性，为精细操作和神经肌肉康复提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人外手套通常依赖标准化测量，缺乏个体化适配，限制了其在精细操作和康复中的效果。

Method: 采用拓扑扫描获取用户手部解剖数据，通过硅胶模具铸造制造个性化手套；运用有限元分析评估驱动器的弯曲行为及物理人机交互接触力；通过气动压力控制实验实现手指的静态和动态屈曲运动。

Result: 拓扑扫描可实现对手部解剖结构的精确定制；仿真表明个性化设计能够分析人机交互接触力，在非理想压缩条件下仍可实现充分的关节活动；气动测试验证了压力控制可精准驱动MCP和PIP关节；放松应变限制层可改善驱动器与手指关节的对齐。

Conclusion: 本工作在结构适形性、关节拓扑、人机接触建模及时间相关驱动-变形特性方面实现了对手部的个性化适配，为优化外手套设计以支持精细操作和神经肌肉康复奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft robotic exogloves can provide hand rehabilitation and assistance. Fitting these gloves often relies on standardized measurements not tailored to the individual, limiting their effectiveness, especially for fine articulation necessary for dexterous manipulation. We present the design, fabrication, modeling, and testing of a personalized pneumatically-actuated soft robotic exoglove. The glove was fit to a user's hand with topological scans and fabricated with silicone mold casting. Finite element analysis (FEA) was performed to evaluate actuator bending and forces from physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) between an actuator and a simplified personalized biomechanical finger model. Pneumatic pressure control experiments were conducted to flex the user's finger with static and dynamic references. Fabrication results show that topological scans enable precise tailoring to hand anatomy. Simulations showed that anatomical personalization enables analysis of pHRI contact forces, and results indicate sufficient joint mobilization with non-ideal compression on the proximal phalanx. Pneumatic testing indicates that pressure control allows accurate and targeted mobility of the metacarpophalangeal (MCP) and proximal interphalangeal (PIP) joints with intrinsic stiffness. Testing of multiple designs showed that relaxing the strain-limiting layer improves actuator-to-finger joint alignment during actuation. This work presents personalization to the human hand in structural conformability, joint topology, modeling of pHRI contact, and time-dependent actuation-deformation profiles. This lays a groundwork for informing exoglove design optimization to enable assistance in dexterous manipulation and neuromuscular rehabilitation of fine motor skills.

</details>


### [82] [In vivo feasibility study of humanoid robots in surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07972)
*Zekai Liang,Nikita Thareja,Peihan Zhang,Calvin Joyce,Soofiyan Atar,Florian Richter,Garth Jacobsen,Shanglei Liu,Ryan Broderick,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前人形机器人在腹腔镜手术任务中的表现，通过台架测试、干实验室用户研究和活体猪实验，量化了技术可行性、任务性能和临床准备度，指出人形机器人在外科应用中既有潜力也面临关键技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保健领域面临人员短缺和护理需求增加的矛盾，而医院大量工作仍需要具身操作（移动、操作、安全交互）。尽管自动化已在数字和后勤任务中应用，但手术等精细操作仍需机器人辅助。人形机器人具有独特潜力，但当前其是否满足微创手术的精度、控制和安全要求尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了一套基于人形机器人的腹腔镜遥操作框架，使用通用手术器械；通过台架特性测试、涵盖不同手术经验水平的干实验室用户研究以及活体猪实验进行系统评估。

Result: 量化了人形机器人在腹腔镜手术任务中的技术可行性、任务性能和临床准备度，并与现有成熟手术平台进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: 人形机器人在外科手术应用中展现出了潜力，但同时存在若干关键技术挑战，在临床部署前必须加以解决。

Abstract: Recent advances in actuation, control and learning have rapidly pushed humanoid robots from a distant vision towards near-term real-world deployment. Healthcare is a particularly pressing domain, in which staffing shortages and increasing care demand are widening the gap between clinical workload and available skilled labour. Although current automation has largely focused on digital and logistical tasks, much hospital work remains embodied, requiring mobility, manipulation and safe interaction in human-designed environments. Humanoid form factors offer unique potential, particularly for assisting with surgical tasks. Traditionally, robotic systems for surgery are purpose-built platforms such as Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci Surgical System, and it remains unclear how close current humanoid systems are to meeting the precision, control and safety requirements of minimally invasive surgery. Here we present a systematic evaluation of contemporary humanoid technology for laparoscopic surgical tasks. We develop a humanoid-based laparoscopic teleoperation framework using general-purpose instruments and assess its abilities through benchtop characterization, dry-laboratory user studies spanning diverse surgical experience levels and in vivo porcine studies. Across these evaluations, we quantify technical feasibility, task performance and clinical readiness relative to established surgical platforms. Together, our study provides an evidence-based assessment of current humanoid abilities and limitations for surgical applications, highlighting both their promise and key technical challenges that must be addressed before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [83] [D-CLIPSE: Distributed Consensus-based Localization with Passive Listening on Shared State Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07995)
*Kyle Biron-Gricken,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共识的分布式滤波框架，通过共享预积分里程计和相关共享状态，在通信高效的前提下实现接近集中式滤波的精度和一致性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式滤波方法虽然能最优融合所有传感器测量，但由于硬件、通信和计算限制难以实际部署；现有分布式方法在精度和一致性方面仍有不足。

Method: 提出一种基于共识的分布式滤波框架，在通信机器人之间共享预积分里程计和相关共享状态，同时保持通信高效性。

Result: 在仿真和实验场景中验证，所提方法在精度和尤其是一致性方面接近集中式性能，优于当前最先进的分布式方法。

Conclusion: 该分布式滤波框架能够有效平衡通信效率与估计性能，为多机器人定位提供了一种实用且一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot localization that is accurate and consistent is imperative for downstream tasks such as planning and control. Centralized filtering approaches optimally fuse all available sensor measurements of the team. However, a centralized solution is rarely implementable due to hardware, communication, and computational constraints. Distributed approaches deploy a filter on each robot to estimate their own state and neighbours' states using inter-robot communication. This paper proposes a consistent, communication-efficient, and consensus-based distributed filtering framework that shares both preintegrated odometry and relevant shared states among communicating robots. The proposed method is validated in simulated and experimental scenarios, showing near centralized performance in accuracy, and especially in consistency, compared to the current state-of-the-art decentralized approach.

</details>


### [84] [Factors Influencing Conversational Engagement in Robot-Delivered Individual Cognitive Stimulation Therapy (iCST) for Dementia in Home Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07998)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人辅助认知刺激疗法（iCST）中与痴呆症患者的对话动态，发现个性化提示能显著提升参与度，会话后期会出现认知疲劳迹象，且首次会话指标可预测长期参与情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人为痴呆症护理中的认知疗法提供了支持手段，但机器人递送的认知刺激疗法（CST）会话中出现的对话动态尚不明确。

Method: 将Co-STAR系统部署在8位痴呆症患者家中一周，完成30分钟会话；分析了每轮词数、语速、响应时长、响应延迟和自我参照语言等会话指标，考察提示个性化、交互阶段和参与者特征的影响。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)个性化提示显著增加响应时长、自我参照语言和整体参与度；(2)会话后期言语输出和自传体参与减少，提示认知疲劳；(3)首次会话指标与长期参与相关，居住状况影响参与模式。

Conclusion: 这些发现为机器人递送的iCST中影响会话参与的因素提供了实证依据，有助于设计适应性的痴呆症治疗对话机器人。

Abstract: Social robots offer a promising means of supporting cognitive therapies for dementia care by guiding structured conversation and therapeutic activities. However, little is known about the conversational dynamics that emerge during robot-delivered cognitive stimulation therapy (CST) sessions. This study analysed the interaction patterns from robot-delivered individual CST (iCST) sessions conducted with people living with dementia in home settings. Our Co-STAR (Cognitive Stimulation Therapy by an Autonomous Robot) system was deployed in the homes of eight PwDs for one week, who completed 30-minute sessions. Conversational metrics, including words per turn, speech production rate, response duration, response latency, and self-referential language, were analysed to examine how conversational engagement is shaped by prompt personalisation, interaction phase, and participant characteristics. The findings highlight three key interactional properties of robot-delivered iCST. First, personalised prompts significantly increase response duration, self-referential language, and overall engagement compared to generic prompts. Second, conversational behaviour changes within sessions, with a reduction in the verbal output and autobiographical engagement observed during later interaction phases, which suggests cognitive fatigue. Third, first-session conversational metrics were associated with long-term participation, while living situation influenced conversational engagement patterns. These findings provide empirical insights into the factors that shape conversational engagement in robot-delivered iCST. They inform the design of adaptive conversational robots for dementia therapy.

</details>


### [85] [RadLoc: Radar-based 3-DoF Global Localization via Fast, Robust, and Lightweight Spatial Descriptor Across Diverse Environmental Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08115)
*Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jungwoo Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RadLoc是一个基于旋转雷达的端到端全局定位流水线，从地点识别到3自由度位姿估计，具有快速、鲁棒和轻量级的特点。它通过1D CA-CFAR预加速处理、利用近距优势设计紧凑描述子和分层粗到精检索策略，结合相位相关位姿估计，在5个数据集15个序列上实现了最小描述子尺寸和最快检索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转雷达的全局定位研究多聚焦于地点识别或位姿估计等单一组件，缺乏从地点识别到位姿估计的完整端到端方案。

Method: 1) 使用1D CA-CFAR滤波加速预处理；2) 利用旋转雷达图像近距优势设计紧凑描述子；3) 采用分层粗到精检索策略；4) 结合相位相关进行3自由度位姿估计。

Result: 在5个数据集的15个序列上，RadLoc在保持鲁棒性能的同时，实现了最小的描述子尺寸和最快的检索时间，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RadLoc是一个适用于SLAM和多会话SLAM系统的通用全局定位模块，在恶劣天气和挑战性环境下表现出色。

Abstract: While global localization using spinning radar has gained attention for its robustness to adverse weather and challenging environments, many studies have focused on individual components such as place recognition or pose estimation. In this paper, we take a holistic view of radar sensor-based global localization and present RadLoc, a fast, robust, and lightweight end-to-end pipeline from place recognition to 3-DoF pose estimation. RadLoc accelerates pre-processing using 1D CA-CFAR filtering and leverages the near-range dominance in spinning radar images to design a compact descriptor and an efficient hierarchical coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy. Moreover, coupled with phase correlation-based 3-DoF pose estimation, it forms a versatile global localization module applicable to SLAM and multi-session SLAM systems. Extensive experiments on 15 sequences across 5 datasets demonstrate that RadLoc achieves robust performance while maintaining the smallest descriptor size and fastest retrieval time among state-of-the-art approaches. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/radloc/.

</details>


### [86] [X-ACTA: eXtended Analytic Center Tension distribution Algorithm for fixed and mobile cable-driven-parallel-robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08265)
*Domenico Dona',Vincenzo Di Paola,Alberto Trevisani,Matteo Zoppi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展解析中心法，用于在缆索驱动并联机器人的力可行工作空间外选择缆索张力，该方法保持可微性、包含非线性约束、确保实时收敛，并通过帕累托优势验证了其在平滑性和力误差方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CDPR的驱动超出力可行工作空间（WFW）可增强其在激进机动或缆索故障等挑战场景中的能力，但现有方法很少能有效处理这些场景。

Method: 提出解析中心法的扩展版本作为WFW外的缆索张力选择准则，保持可微性并包含非线性约束；通过帕累托优势与最先进方法对比。

Result: 该方法保持连续可微的张力分布，确保快速实时收敛到唯一解；与基于松弛的其他方法相比，将力误差限制在WFW的可忽略区域；在平滑性和力误差方面通过帕累托优势优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在WFW外选择缆索张力方面具有平滑性、实时性和低力误差的优势，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Abstract: Steering Cable-Driven Parallel Robots (CDPRs) beyond their Wrench-Feasible Workspace (WFW) augments their capabilities in challenging scenarios such as during aggressive maneuvers or following a cable failure. In this context, although the determination of cable tensions is a well-studied topic, only a few approaches address these scenarios. Therefore, this paper introduces an extended version of the Analytic Center method as a criterion for selecting cable tensions outside the WFW while maintaining differentiability and including non-linear constraints. Notably, the proposed method maintains continuous and differentiable tension profiles, ensures fast real-time convergence to a unique solution, and, in contrast to other slack-based formulations, relegates wrench errors to a negligible area of the WFW. Its superiority in terms of smoothness and wrench error is confirmed via Pareto dominance with respect to the leading state-of-the-art method. Lastly, the effectiveness of the method is demonstrated through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [87] [TFP: Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies for Visuomotor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08283)
*Yushen Liang,Yue Peng,Baosheng Jin,Tianluo Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Shuyi Zhou,Zhuoran Chen,Xinqi Liu,Shenji Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级记忆-动作框架TFP，通过液体时间常数动力学维护任务进度信念并注入动作解码器，使VLA策略具备动态感知信念更新能力，在LIBERO、LIBERO-plus和MIKASA ShellGameTouch等基准上显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略（如π₀.₅和OpenVLA）本质上是反应式的，即仅从当前观测、指令和本体感知状态预测下一动作。这在家务操作中视觉相似状态可能需要不同动作的阶段依赖任务（如潜伏任务进度、先前交互结果）中会失效。因此需要不仅具有记忆，还要具备动态感知信念更新的策略。

Method: TFP框架包含：(1) 一个基于液体时间常数（Liquid Time-Constant）动力学的任务进度信念维护模块，能够稳定相位保持信念、接触/释放/子目标过渡附近修正信念；(2) 将更新后的信念通过自适应调制（adaptive modulation）直接注入流匹配动作解码器，使时间累积的上下文直接塑造生成的动作块，而非仅作为被动历史上下文。

Result: 使用3.3B参数模型，TFP在LIBERO上将平均成功率从96.9%提升至98.75%，在LIBERO-plus上从91.4%提升至93.77%，在记忆聚焦的MIKASA ShellGameTouch诊断任务上达到75.0%的成功率。机制分析显示操作事件附近的写增益变化约为非事件阶段的6倍，隐状态干预实验证明信念因果地调制生成的动作块。

Conclusion: 紧凑的事件敏感记忆动力学能够在遮挡、视觉扰动和阶段依赖任务结构下有效改进VLA策略，使其超越纯反应式行为。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) policies such as $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA perform well on many manipulation tasks, but they are often reactive: the next action is predicted from the current observation, instruction, and proprioceptive state. This assumption breaks down in stage-dependent manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on latent task progress and previous interaction outcomes. We argue that such tasks require not only memory, but dynamics-aware belief updates: the policy should preserve task progress during stable or occluded phases and revise its belief near contact, release, or subgoal transitions. We introduce Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies (TFP), a lightweight memory-action framework for VLA backbones. TFP maintains an episode-local task-progress belief with Liquid Time-Constant dynamics and injects the updated belief directly into the flow-matching action decoder through adaptive modulation. This lets temporally accumulated context shape the generated action chunk, rather than serving only as passive history context. With a 3.3B-parameter model, TFP improves the average success rate from \(96.9\%\) to \(98.75\%\) on LIBERO and from \(91.4\%\) to \(93.77\%\) on LIBERO-plus. On the memory-focused MIKASA ShellGameTouch diagnostic, TFP achieves success up to \(75.0\%\). Mechanistic analyses show that write-gain changes near manipulation events are about \(6\times\) larger than far non-event phases, and hidden-state interventions show that the belief causally modulates generated action chunks. These results suggest that compact, event-sensitive memory dynamics can improve VLA policies under occlusion, visual perturbation, and stage-dependent task structure.

</details>


### [88] [AnyDexRT: Calibration-Free Dexterous Hand Retargeting with Few-Shot Human Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08341)
*Chenxi Wang,Ying Feng,Hongjie Fang,Shangning Xia,Lixin Yang,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyDexRT是一种无需标定的灵巧手遥操作重定向方法，通过自监督指尖对应学习、少样本人类引导和接触分类器优化，在多种灵巧手上实现了比现有方法更优的重定向质量和操控效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手遥操作中的运动学重定向方法依赖手工设计的优化目标、精确标定或全局形状匹配，对不同灵巧手需要大量针对性的手动调参，缺乏通用性和可靠性。

Method: AnyDexRT结合自监督指尖对应学习与少样本人类引导，将映射锚定在任务相关区域，并利用接触分类器进一步优化捏合相关姿态。

Result: 在多种灵巧手和真实遥操作任务中，AnyDexRT提升了重定向质量，减少了手动调参，比先前方法提供了更直观高效的控制。

Conclusion: AnyDexRT提出了一种无需标定、跨灵巧手通用且直觉化的遥操作重定向方案，显著降低了部署成本并提升了控制性能。

Abstract: Teleoperation is a key interface for controlling dexterous robotic hands and collecting demonstrations for imitation learning. Its effectiveness largely depends on kinematic retargeting, which maps operator hand motions to feasible and intuitive robot hand motions. Existing methods often require hand-crafted objectives, precise calibration, or global shape matching between human and robot hand spaces, making them sensitive to hand-specific tuning and less reliable across different dexterous hands. We propose AnyDexRT, a calibration-free retargeting method for intuitive dexterous teleoperation across human-like dexterous hands. AnyDexRT combines self-supervised fingertip correspondence learning with few-shot human guidance to anchor the mapping in task-relevant regions, and further refines pinch-related poses using a contact classifier. Experiments on diverse dexterous hands and real-world teleoperation tasks show that AnyDexRT improves retargeting quality, reduces manual tuning, and provides more intuitive and efficient control than prior retargeting methods. Project website: https://chenxi-wang.github.io/projects/anydexrt

</details>


### [89] [SkillPlug: Unsupervised Skill Mining for Few-Shot Adaptation in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08354)
*Zi-han Ding,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillPlug是一个插件式框架，通过技能条件模块和自监督学习的可迁移技能库，增强现有视觉运动策略，实现跨操作任务的少样本泛化和高效适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略大多端到端训练，缺乏用于跨任务复用和重组行为的显式结构，导致在有限监督下迁移效率低下。

Method: 提出SkillPlug框架：1）在现有视觉运动策略中插入技能条件模块；2）通过自监督目标从多任务演示中挖掘紧凑、可复用、非冗余的技能库；3）保持技能固定，仅微调轻量级路由器和动作头以适应新任务。

Result: 在两项仿真基准和真实机器人上，SkillPlug挖掘的可迁移技能一致地提升了多任务性能和少样本适应能力。

Conclusion: SkillPlug提供了一种可扩展的方式来挖掘可复用技能，提高了机器人操作中数据高效的泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning transferable visuomotor imitation policies that generalize across diverse manipulation tasks and adapt rapidly to new tasks from only a handful of demonstrations remains challenging. Most modern policies are trained end-to-end to map observations directly to low-level actions, offering little explicit structure for reusing and recombining behaviors across tasks and making transfer data-inefficient under limited supervision. We propose SkillPlug, a plug-in framework that augments an existing visuomotor policy with a skill-conditioning module and mines a shared, transferable skill library from raw multi-task demonstrations. SkillPlug learns skills via self-supervised objectives that promote compact, reusable, and non-redundant behavior-level primitives, forming a task-shared prior for compositional control. After skill mining, we keep the learned skills fixed and specialize to unseen tasks by fine-tuning only lightweight router and action head, enabling efficient adaptation without full end-to-end retraining. We evaluate SkillPlug on two simulation benchmarks and on a real robot, and observe that the mined transferable skills consistently improve both multi-task performance and few-shot adaptation. Overall, SkillPlug offers a scalable way to mine reusable skills that improve data-efficient generalization in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [90] [FSD-VLN: Fast-Slow Dual-System Modeling for Aerial Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08359)
*Xueke Zhu,Qingyan Meng,Liutao Yu,Wei Zhang,Zhengyu Ma,Huihui Zhou,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FSD-VLN快慢双系统架构，通过解耦语义推理与飞行指令生成，在无人机视觉语言导航任务中相比SOTA方法成功率提升2倍，推理延迟和总任务耗时降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法中全局多模态理解与序列动作生成存在结构错配，导致长航时导航轨迹抖动和严重决策延迟。

Method: 提出快慢双系统架构（FSD-VLN）：慢流提取预训练视觉语言模型的稳定语义先验，快流使用扩散Transformer（DiT）建模跨时序动作分布以生成一致飞行输出，并引入时间感知自适应优化器稳定长序列训练。

Result: 大规模低空仿真实验中，在未见场景下导航成功率相比SOTA方法提升2倍，单动作推理延迟和总任务耗时降低超过50%。

Conclusion: 验证了解耦语义-控制建模的有效性，为长航时空中VLN提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables UAV autonomous navigation in unknown environments by mapping language instructions to real-time visual inputs. Compared with GPS-dependent or pre-programmed navigation, VLN supports intuitive human-machine interaction and stronger environmental adaptability, requiring tight integration of high-level semantic reasoning and low-latency flight control.Existing methods suffer from structural misalignment between global multimodal understanding and sequential action generation, causing jittery trajectories and severe decision latency for long-horizon aerial navigation. To solve this issue, we propose FSD-VLN, a fast-slow dual-system architecture disentangling semantic reasoning and low-latency flight command generation.The framework has two asynchronous branches: a slow stream extracting stable semantic priors from pre-trained vision-language models, and a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) fast stream modeling cross-temporal action distributions to produce consistent flight outputs. We further introduce a time-aware adaptive optimizer to stabilize long-sequence training and reduce gradient oscillation.Large-scale low-altitude simulation experiments show FSD-VLN achieves up to 2X higher navigation success rates on unseen scenes than SOTA methods, while cutting single-action inference delay and total task runtime by over 50%. Our work validates the benefit of decoupled semantic-control modeling and provides a practical paradigm for long-horizon aerial VLN.

</details>


### [91] [On Exploring Input Resolution Scaling For Anytime LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08391)
*Ahmet Soyyigit,Shuochao Yao,Heechul Yun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多分辨率推理的任意时刻计算方法，使处理LiDAR点云的DNN模型能够动态调整输入分辨率以平衡执行延迟与结果效用，仅需部署单一模型并配合截止时间感知调度器，在nuScenes数据集和自动驾驶仿真中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN在面向LiDAR点云的3D目标检测中缺乏动态调整执行延迟与结果质量的能力，无法适应动态变化的运行时需求。

Method: 提出多分辨率推理方法，让处理点云pillar/voxel的模型支持动态分辨率输入（仅需单一模型）；设计截止时间感知调度器，通过精确预测不同分辨率下的执行时间，为每个输入选择最高可达分辨率。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上显著优于现有任意时刻计算方法；在仿真自动驾驶系统中实现无碰撞导航，避免不必要的停顿。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效实现了LiDAR点云DNN的任意时刻计算，兼顾系统实时性与检测精度，适用于资源受限或任务动态变化的自动驾驶等场景。

Abstract: Making tradeoffs between execution latency and result utility (i.e., anytime computing) for adapting to dynamic operational requirements has been shown to enhance the performance of cyber-physical systems. In this work, we focus on enabling anytime computing for deep neural networks (DNNs) that process LiDAR point clouds for 3D object detection. We propose a novel method that enables multi-resolution inference for models that process point clouds as pillars or voxels, allowing the input to be dynamically scaled and processed at the resolution needed to meet timing requirements. Importantly, our memory-efficient approach requires the deployment of only a single DNN model, avoiding the need to deploy multiple models, each trained for a different input resolution. We also introduce a deadline-aware scheduler that selects the highest possible resolution for any given input by accurately predicting the execution time for all possible resolutions at runtime, which is challenging due to the irregularity of LiDAR point clouds. Experimental results on the nuScenes autonomous driving dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing anytime computing approaches for LiDAR object detection. Finally, we deploy our approach in a simulated autonomous driving system, where it consistently enables collision-free navigation while avoiding unnecessary stalls caused by environmental complexity.

</details>


### [92] [EgoWAM: World Action Models Beyond Pixels with In-the-Wild Egocentric Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08436)
*Baoyu Li,Xinchen Yin,Mengying Lin,Yixin Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用第一人称人类数据训练机器人操作策略，提出EgoWAM框架，通过预测世界状态（而非仅动作）实现人-机器人迁移。实验表明，使用DINO和3D运动流作为世界表示比像素预测和行为克隆效果更好，显著提升泛化能力与域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心的人体数据为机器人操作提供了可扩展的监督信号，但行为克隆会将可迁移内容（物体、场景、任务语义）与不可迁移因素（人体形态、头部运动、行为风格）纠缠在一起。现有方法难以有效分离这些因素，限制了人类数据向机器人策略的迁移效率。

Method: 提出EgoWAM框架，基于World Action Models (WAMs)思想，要求策略不仅预测动作，还预测场景如何演变。核心假设是有效的世界目标应抽象外观、捕获与智能体无关的物理效应，并将相机运动与环境变化分离。在保证策略骨干、动作头和数据集混合不变的前提下，比较三种世界预测目标：Pixel（像素级）、DINO（自监督视觉特征）和3D运动流。

Result: 在三个真实世界双臂任务上，WAM联合训练比行为克隆更有效地利用野外自我中心人类数据。像素级预测迁移效果弱；DINO在物体和场景的分布外泛化上提升高达4倍；3D流在域内性能上提升20-30%。

Conclusion: 世界动作模型（WAM）为人类到机器人的行为迁移提供了比行为克隆更好的训练信号，其中DINO和3D运动流作为世界表示分别擅长分布外泛化和域内性能提升，验证了选择合适的世界抽象对跨形态迁移至关重要。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offers scalable supervision for robot manipulation. However, behavior cloning entangles transferable content like objects, scenes, and task semantics, with non-transferable factors like human morphology, head motion, and behavioral style. We study whether World Action Models (WAMs) provide a better training signal by requiring policies to predict not only actions, but also how the scene evolves. The central question is what world representation best enables human-to-robot transfer. We hypothesize that an effective world target should abstract appearance, capture agent-invariant physical effects, and separate camera motion from environment change. We introduce EgoWAM, a controlled human-robot co-training framework that fixes the policy backbone, action head, and data mixture while varying only the world prediction target, comparing Pixel, DINO, and 3D motion flow. Across three real-world bimanual tasks, WAM co-training scales more effectively with in-the-wild egocentric human data than behavior cloning. Pixel-based prediction transfers weakly, while DINO and 3D flow yield substantial gains: DINO improves out-of-distribution object and scene generalization by up to 4x, and 3D flow improves in-domain performance by 20-30%. More details: https://gatech-rl2.github.io/egowam.github.io

</details>


### [93] [Harness VLA: Steering Frozen VLAs into Reliable Manipulation Primitives via Memory-Guided Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08448)
*Yixian Zhang,Huanming Zhang,Feng Gao,Xiao Li,Zhihao Liu,Chunyang Zhu,Jiaxing Qiu,Yuchen Yan,Jiyuan Liu,Wenhao Tang,Zhengru Fang,Yi Nie,Changxu Wei,Yu Wang,Wenbo Ding,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Harness VLA 提出一种记忆增强的智能体框架，将冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）作为可重试的接触式基元，与少量分析性基元组合，通过任务执行轨迹学习固定基元的操作范围，在不微调的情况下将预训练VLA扩展到其原始轨迹分布之外，在多个扰动操作基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLA模型在分布内任务轨迹上表现良好，但在部署扰动（如语义重定向、目标重新绑定、空间布局变化、不稳定局部接触）下容易失败；而LLM编码智能体虽提供语义和组合推理能力，但纯分析性基元在处理不规则抓取、受限放置和铰接物体交互时存在困难。

Method: 提出Harness VLA框架：1）将冻结的VLA作为可重试的接触式基元；2）与少量固定分析性基元（用于接地、暂存、运输、导航、释放）组合；3）通过学习固定基元的操作范围（来自任务执行轨迹、全局成功规则和失败模型）而非扩展技能库；4）将语义重新接地、非接触执行和VLA重新暂存提升到规划器层面，保留冻结VLA仅用于局部接触阶段。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro上提升38.6个百分点，在RoboCasa365上提升25.4个百分点，在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%。

Conclusion: 无需微调即可将预训练VLA扩展到其原始轨迹分布之外，有效处理部署扰动，在多种操作场景（桌面、厨房、双臂操作）中均表现优异。

Abstract: Language-conditioned manipulation requires both precise contact-rich control and robust reasoning over language, scenes, and long horizons. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong local visuomotor skills, but they are trained on in-distribution task trajectories and often fail under deployment perturbations such as semantic retargeting, goal re-binding, spatial-layout shifts, and unstable local contacts. LLM coding agents provide complementary semantic and compositional reasoning, but purely analytic primitives struggle with irregular grasping, constrained placement, and articulated-object interaction. We present Harness VLA, a memory-augmented agentic framework that exposes a frozen VLA as a retryable contact-rich primitive and composes it with a small fixed library of analytic primitives for grounding, staging, transport, navigation, and release. Rather than expanding the skill library, the harness learns the operating range of these fixed primitives from task-specific execution traces, global success rules, and failure models. By lifting semantic re-grounding, non-contact execution, and VLA re-staging to the planner while reserving the frozen VLA for local contact-rich phases, Harness VLA extends pretrained VLAs beyond their original trajectory distribution without finetuning. Across perturbed tabletop, household kitchen, and clean-to-randomized bimanual manipulation, Harness VLA improves over the strongest relevant baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and reaches 58.4% on RoboTwin C2R.

</details>


### [94] [FabriVLA: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08575)
*Shiyuan Yang,Borong Zhang,Jizheng Zhang,Zhijia Tao,Junfei Guo,Donglai Ran,Xu Bian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FabriVLA是一个轻量级视觉-语言-动作模型，基于1B规模VLM和流匹配动作头，在Meta-World MT50的50个操作任务上达到90.0%的平均成功率，证明紧凑型VLA无需数十亿参数也能实现强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖数十亿参数的大规模骨干网络，计算成本高且部署困难，本文旨在探索轻量级VLA能否在复杂多任务操作场景下达到同等水平的性能。

Method: FabriVLA采用InternVL3.5视觉-语言骨干网络，结合流匹配动作头（包含动作token间的门控自注意力与浅层VLM层融合以增强空间上下文），通过单阶段联合优化从预训练VLM和随机初始化动作头进行训练。

Result: 在Meta-World MT50基准（50个多样化操作任务）上，FabriVLA的层级平均成功率达到90.0%。

Conclusion: 基于1B规模VLM构建的紧凑型VLA能够在多任务精细操作中取得强性能，无需依赖数十亿参数的大型VLA骨干网络。

Abstract: We present FabriVLA, a lightweight Vision-Language-Action model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation. FabriVLA combines an InternVL3.5 vision-language backbone with a flow-matching action head featuring gated self-attention across action tokens and shallow VLM layer fusion for enriched spatial context. The model is trained via single stage joint optimization from a pretrained VLM and randomly initialized action head. On the Meta-World MT50 benchmark spanning 50 diverse manipulation tasks, FabriVLA achieves a tier-average success rate of 90.0%, demonstrating that a compact VLA built on a 1B scale VLM can achieve strong performance without relying on multi billion parameter VLA backbones.

</details>


### [95] [A New Human-Likeness and Comfort Index for Robot Movements Along Prescribed Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08620)
*Rosanna Coccaro,Enrico Ferrentino,Antonio Parziale,Angelo Marcelli,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于人类运动的对数正态性原理，定义了一个类人指数来预先表征轨迹的类人程度，并通过68名受试者的物理交互实验验证了该指数与感知舒适度之间存在一致的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的视觉相似性不足以保证物理交互中的接受度，而接受度与人类感知的舒适度和人体工程学直接相关，因此需要一种能预先评估机器人运动类人程度（进而评估舒适度）的量化指标。

Method: 基于人类运动建模的对数正态性原理，定义了类人指数（human-likeness index），用于在轨迹实际执行前对轨迹生成算法进行先验表征和评估。通过68名受试者的三项物理交互实验，验证该指数与感知舒适度的一致性趋势。

Result: 三项实验结果表明，感知舒适度的偏好分布与所提出的类人指数分布之间存在全局一致的趋势。

Conclusion: 所提出的类人指数可作为评估轨迹生成算法是否产生类人运动的有效先验工具，且该指数与人类在物理交互中的舒适度感知高度相关。

Abstract: As human-robot interaction rapidly spreads in numerous fields, the subject of robot acceptance gains increasing importance. Visual similarity to the human body, as occurs for humanoids, is generally not enough to ensure acceptance in physical interaction, as acceptance directly links to comfort and ergonomics, which are measured in terms of the quality of the robot movement perceived by the human. This paper discusses the connection between comfort and similarity of the robot movement to the human one. By considering the kinematic characterization of human movement, this paper focuses on the time laws of such movements, wherein the end-effector path is prescribed. Based on the lognormality principle for modeling human movements, a human-likeness index is defined and used to provide an a priori characterization of trajectories. Such an index can be used to evaluate the performance of trajectory generation algorithms in producing human-like movements before they are actually executed. For validation purposes, 68 subjects are required to judge their comfort. The results of three experimental campaigns involving a physical interaction with a robot demonstrate a globally consistent trend between the preference in terms of perceived comfort and the distribution of the suggested human-likeness index.

</details>


### [96] [Native Video-Action Pretraining for Generalizable Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08639)
*Qihang Zhang,Lin Li,Luyao Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Shuaiting Li,Ruilin Wang,Junke Wang,Jiahao Shao,Gangwei Xu,Jiaming Zhou,Yishu Shen,Yudong Jin,Fangyi Xu,Shuailei Ma,Jiaqi Liao,Guanxing Lu,Zifan Shi,Yongkun Wen,Yujie Zhao,Weixuan Tang,Xinyang Wang,Chaojian Li,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Nan Xue,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-VA 2.0 是一种面向具身智能的视频-动作基础模型，通过四项核心设计（语义视觉-动作分词器、因果预训练、稀疏MoE架构、异步推理方案）解决了视频生成模型用于物理环境控制时的根本性不足，在复杂操作任务上展现出强大的少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型本质上是为数字内容创作设计的，无法直接适用于物理环境中的机器人控制。作者旨在构建一个从底层为具身智能设计的视频-动作基础模型，弥补视频生成与物理世界交互之间的鸿沟。

Method: (1) 提出语义视觉-动作分词器，替代传统重建驱动的VAE，将视觉表示与语义和动作对齐；(2) 采用因果预训练范式，从头训练以避免双向架构适配时的灾难性遗忘；(3) 使用稀疏MoE骨干网络，在保持效率的同时扩大模型容量；(4) 通过异步推理方案实现实时闭环控制，在动作执行的同时并行预测未来潜在表示，并通过学习的前向动力学在每次rollout中基于最新观测重新接地。

Result: 真实世界部署验证了LingBot-VA 2.0作为鲁棒基础模型的有效性，在复杂操作任务上展现出少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 重新审视了视频生成模型用于物理环境控制的根本局限性，提出了四项面向具身智能的设计原则，实验证明了该模型在真实机器人控制中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: The advent of video-action models offers a promising path for robot control. Nevertheless, we argue that repurposing video generative models designed for digital content creation is inherently inadequate for physical environments. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model built from the ground up for embodiment. Four core design principles showcase its evolution from LingBot-VA. (1) Departing from traditional reconstruction-focused VAEs, we introduce a semantic visual-action tokenizer, which aligns visual representations with both semantics and actions, improving instruction following and action precision in subsequent policy learning. (2) Given the strictly causal nature of temporal dynamics, we adopt a causal pretraining paradigm, training from scratch to circumvent the catastrophic forgetting that frequently occurs when adapting bidirectional architectures. (3) To meet the demands of high-frequency inference, our model employs a sparse MoE backbone, expanding model capacity without compromising efficiency. (4) Real-time closed-loop control is realized through an enhanced asynchronous inference scheme, which predicts future latents in parallel with action execution while re-grounding each rollout on the latest observation via learned forward dynamics. Real-world deployment validates LingBot-VA 2.0 as a robust foundation model, as evidenced by its few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Adaptive Solvers for Distributed Factor Graph Optimization on Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08735)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepCORD是一个学习增强的分布式因子图优化框架，通过将并行Riemannian优化器展开为可微迭代，学习自适应反馈策略，在矩阵Lie群上实现同步和异步通信模式下的分布式优化，在SE(3)位姿图优化和SL(4)投影子图对齐任务上优于现有分布式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式求解器依赖脆弱的手工调参，且主要针对刚体位姿图，缺乏对一般矩阵Lie群上分布式优化的通用解决方案。

Method: 将并行加速的Riemannian优化器展开为可微迭代，学习自监督反馈策略，根据优化阶段和通信状态动态调整求解器参数，支持同步和异步通信。

Result: 在SE(3)位姿图优化和SL(4)投影子图对齐基准测试中，在大多数现实场景下取得了比现有分布式基线更低的优化目标值。

Conclusion: DeepCORD通过学习自适应参数调节策略，有效解决了分布式因子图优化中手工调参的弊端，在多个矩阵Lie群优化任务上表现优越。

Abstract: Modern robotic perception increasingly involves large-scale geometric optimization problems distributed across multiple robots or sessions. However, existing distributed solvers often depend on brittle hand tuning and primarily target rigid body pose graphs. To address this, we present DeepCORD, a learning-augmented framework for distributed factor graph optimization on general matrix Lie groups. By unfolding a parallel and accelerated Riemannian optimizer into differentiable iterations, DeepCORD learns a self-supervised feedback policy that dynamically adapts solver parameters according to the optimization phase and communication status. The resulting method enables adaptive distributed optimization over matrix Lie groups under both synchronous and asynchronous communication regimes. Extensive experiments on real-world $\mathrm{SE}$(3) pose graph optimization and $\mathrm{SL}$(4) projective submap alignment show that our method achieves lower objective values than existing distributed baselines on most benchmarks across realistic operating scenarios.

</details>


### [98] [ContactMimic: Humanoid Object Interaction via Contact Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08742)
*Xinyao Li,Xialin He,Runpei Dong,Saurabh Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CONTACTMIMIC是一个通过显式跟踪零件级二元接触指令和关键点轨迹来实现机器人精确物理接触与接触可控性的学习框架，在10种人-物交互仿真和5种真实场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠关键点跟踪不足以完成需要有意义物理接触的对象交互任务（如坐椅子、擦白板、推家具），机器人可能达到正确姿态却未与物体产生有效接触。

Method: 提出CONTACTMIMIC框架，包含接触跟随奖励和轨迹增强方案，以打破关键点轨迹与接触标签之间的相关性，从而解耦接触行为与关键点几何信息。

Result: 在10种多样化人-物交互仿真中，CONTACTMIMIC无需任务特定奖励即可完成操控任务，并在接触相关任务上优于仅使用关键点的跟踪器；消融实验验证了轨迹增强方案的必要性；Sim2Real部署在5种不同运动上验证了接触可控性。

Conclusion: CONTACTMIMIC成功实现了接触行为与关键点几何的解耦，具备精确的物理接触能力和接触可控性（可在线选择产生或抑制接触），为机器人完成需要物理交互的操控任务提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Keypoint tracking alone is insufficient for object interaction tasks such as sitting on a chair, wiping a board, or pushing furniture, where the robot can reach the correct pose without making meaningful physical contact with the object. We present CONTACTMIMIC, a learning framework that tracks explicit partlevel binary contact commands alongside keypoint trajectories. CONTACTMIMIC is made possible through the use of contact-following rewards and a trajectory augmentation scheme aimed at breaking the correlations between keypoint trajectories and contact labels. The resulting policy successfully decouples contact behavior from keypoint geometry, and achieves precise physical contact as well as contact-controllability (produce or suppress contact during deployment as desired). Simulation experiments across 10 diverse human-object interaction motions confirm that CONTACTMIMIC exhibits contact controllability that enables it to complete manipulation tasks without task-specific rewards, while also outperforming keypoint-only trackers on contact-relevant tasks. Ablations confirm the necessity of the proposed trajectory augmentation scheme and sim2real deployment validates contact controllability in the real world across 5 different motions. Video results are available on https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page/.

</details>


### [99] [DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08751)
*Yunchao Yao,Zhuxiu Xu,Tianqi Zhang,Zixian Liu,Sikai Li,Zhenyu Wei,Feng Chen,Dihong Huang,Kechang Wan,Chenyang Ma,Shuqi Zhao,Shenghua Gao,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yi Ma,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexVerse是一个大规模模块化灵巧操作基准，包含100个任务、3种机械臂和6种灵巧手，支持可配置视觉变化，通过19个任务的基准测试揭示了任务泛化和视觉运动鲁棒性的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在任务和数据多样性、具身覆盖范围或可控视觉变化方面存在局限，阻碍了跨任务和跨具身泛化的研究。

Method: 构建包含100个多样化操作任务、3种机械臂、6种灵巧手的大规模模块化基准DexVerse，提供可配置的纹理、背景、光照和相机视角视觉变化，以及VR遥操作接口和3,180条演示数据。

Result: 在19个任务上对Diffusion Policy、DP3、OpenVLA和π0.5等代表性方法进行基准测试，揭示了任务泛化和视觉运动鲁棒性方面的重大挑战。

Conclusion: DexVerse可作为通用灵巧操作的有前景测试平台，为跨任务和跨具身泛化研究提供支持。

Abstract: Building general-purpose dexterous manipulation policies requires benchmarks that go beyond isolated tasks to systematically evaluate policies across diverse interaction modes, sensory conditions, and robot embodiments. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task and data diversity, embodiment coverage, or controllable visual variation, hindering studies of cross-task and cross-embodiment generalization. We present DexVerse, a large-scale and modular benchmark for dexterous manipulation. DexVerse includes 100 tasks spanning a broad range of manipulation skills, including object grasping and relocation, articulated-object interaction, functional tool use, bimanual coordination, non-prehensile control, contact-rich behaviors, multi-goal execution, and long-horizon multi-stage task completion. It supports 3 robot arms and 6 dexterous hands, and is extensible to new tasks, assets, and embodiments. To evaluate visuomotor generalization, DexVerse provides configurable visual variations in textures, background, lighting, and camera viewpoints. We further provide a VR-based teleoperation interface and 3,180 demonstrations with synchronized proprioceptive, RGB, depth, point-cloud, and state observations. We benchmark representative methods, including Diffusion Policy, DP3, OpenVLA, and $π_{0.5}$, across 19 tasks. Results reveal substantial challenges in task generalization and visuomotor robustness, establishing DexVerse as a promising testbed for general-purpose dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://ycyao216.github.io/DexVerse.site

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Infinite families of Diophantine quadruples in $\mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{-2}]$ in the remaining exceptional congruence classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07838)
*Andrej Dujella,Ivan Soldo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在环ℤ[√-2]中继续研究D(z)-四元组。针对先前分类中遗留的三种例外同余类（24a+5+(12b+6)√-2、24a+2+(12b+6)√-2和48a+44+(24b+12)√-2），通过结合正则扩展方法与固定因子e|3z和小元素v∈ℤ[√-2]的新族，构造了显式的D(z)-四元组，证明每个例外类包含无穷多个可容纳两次半正则D(z)-四元组（即包含两个正则D(z)-三元组）的z值。此外讨论了例外值z∈{-1,1±2√-2}及计算搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成对环ℤ[√-2]中D(z)-四元组的完整分类，解决此前文献中遗留的三种例外同余类中是否存在无穷多个D(z)-四元组的问题。

Method: 采用正则扩展方法，并引入固定因子e|3z和小元素v∈ℤ[√-2]构造新族，系统处理三种例外同余类（24a+5+(12b+6)√-2、24a+2+(12b+6)√-2、48a+44+(24b+12)√-2）的构造问题。

Result: 成功在每个先前未解决的例外同余类中构造出显式的D(z)-四元组，证明每一类均包含无穷多个z值，使得存在一个含有两个正则D(z)-三元组的四元组（即两次半正则D(z)-四元组）。

Conclusion: 彻底解决了环ℤ[√-2]中D(z)-四元组在剩余例外同余类中的存在性问题，完成了该环上D(z)-四元组的分类工作，并对特殊例外值和计算搜索给出额外说明。

Abstract: We continue the study of $D(z)$-quadruples in the ring $\mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{-2}]$. Motivated by the earlier classification due to the authors and by the subsequent partial results for the remaining families, we consider the exceptional congruence classes arising in the forms $24a+5+(12b+6)\sqrt{-2}$, $24a+2+(12b+6)\sqrt{-2}$, and $48a+44+(24b+12)\sqrt{-2}$. By combining the regular extension method with new families obtained by fixing a divisor $e\mid 3z$ and a small element $v\in \mathbb{Z}[\sqrt{-2}]$, we construct explicit $D(z)$-quadruples in each of the previously unsolved congruence classes. More precisely, we show that every exceptional class contains infinitely many values of $z$ admitting a twice semi-regular $D(z)$-quadruple, i.e., a quadruple containing two regular $D(z)$-triples. We also include remarks on the exceptional values $z\in\{-1,1\pm 2\sqrt{-2}\}$ and on a computational search in the exceptional congruence classes.

</details>


### [101] [Non-orthogonality of the cubic and quartic large sieves via Rankin-Selberg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07911)
*Alexandre de Faveri,Alexander Dunn,Jeffrey Hoffstein*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文无条件证明了三次和四次大筛法并非完美正交，主要障碍来自高斯和带来的偏差。证明需要两个关键输入：Kubota Dirichlet级数第二矩的Lindelöf平均上界，以及某类元胞theta函数的Rankin-Selberg卷积傅里叶系数的紧致平均下界。同时提出了关于Hecke特征族算子范数的精确猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高阶大筛法的正交性，特别是三次和四次情形，揭示其不满足完美正交的根本原因。

Method: 采用Zagier(1981)的Rankin-Selberg正则化方法，结合Kubota Dirichlet级数的矩估计和元胞theta函数傅里叶系数的平均下界分析。

Result: 证明了三次和四次大筛法非完美正交，给出了Hecke特征族算子范数的精确猜想。

Conclusion: 对于任意阶n≥3的Hecke特征族，在包含分圆域的数域上均预期不满足完美正交性。

Abstract: We show unconditionally that the cubic and quartic large sieves are not perfectly orthogonal. The main obstruction to perfect orthogonality comes from the bias exhibited by Gauss sums.
  Our proof requires two main inputs: a Lindelöf-on-average upper bound for the second moment of Kubota's Dirichlet series, and a tight average lower bound for the Fourier coefficients of a certain Rankin-Selberg convolution of metaplectic theta functions. The latter input is particularly important in the quartic case, where much less is known about Fourier coefficients of metaplectic theta functions. To establish both of these inputs, we adapt a Rankin-Selberg regularization method due to Zagier (1981).
  In addition to the cubic and quartic cases considered in this paper, we expect that the family of Hecke characters of each fixed order $n \geq 3$ over a number field $K \supset \mathbb{Q}(ζ_n)$ is not perfectly orthogonal. We provide a precise conjecture for the operator norm of these ensembles for each $n$.

</details>


### [102] [Elliptic curves, Fourier ratio, and sampling complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08051)
*W. Burstein,A. Iosevich,A. Sant*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从傅里叶复杂度的角度研究有限域上Legendre椭圆曲线族的标准迹，证明了傅里叶变换的ℓ²范数与系数有特定比例关系，并揭示了傅里叶幅度分布的高复杂度性质，包括存在指数多个具有相同傅里叶幅度但相距甚远的函数，从而在信息论意义上给出了信号重构所需采样数的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从傅里叶复杂度的视角理解Legendre椭圆曲线族在有限域上的归一化Frobenius迹函数f(t)的结构特性，探究其傅里叶表示的稀疏性及信号重构的信息论极限。

Method: 利用Weil混合特征和界、二次高斯和求值以及初等特征恒等式等算术工具，分析f的傅里叶变换的ℓ¹和ℓ²范数之比，构造具有相同傅里叶幅度和傅里叶比例但彼此远离的大族函数。

Result: (1) ‖f̂‖₁/‖f̂‖₂ ≍ √p，即傅里叶变换ℓ²范数为O(p)而单个系数一致有界；(2) 少于O(p)个频率的傅里叶模型无法以固定比例误差逼近f；(3) 存在至少exp(cp)个具有相同傅里叶幅度和比例的实值函数，任意两个ℓ²距离至少c√p，因此用有界精度采样重建该族函数需要至少c_B p个样本。

Conclusion: 该椭圆曲线迹函数的傅里叶表示具有高度非稀疏性，其信息复杂度与p线性相关，且下界不依赖于计算假设，是算术信息论意义上的无条件结论。

Abstract: We study the normalized Frobenius trace associated with the Legendre family of elliptic curves over $\mathbb F_p$ from the point of view of Fourier complexity. If \[ f(t)=\frac{a_p(E_t)}{\sqrt p}, \qquad E_t:\ y^2=x(x-1)(x-t), \] with $f(0)=f(1)=0$, then \[ \frac{\|\widehat f\|_1}{\|\widehat f\|_2}\asymp \sqrt p. \] More precisely, the Fourier transform of $f$ has squared $\ell^2$ norm of order $p$ while its individual coefficients remain uniformly bounded. It follows that no Fourier model supported on fewer than a sufficiently small constant multiple of $p$ frequencies can approximate $f$ in $\ell^2$ with error smaller than a fixed proportion of $\|f\|_2$.
  We also show that the Fourier magnitude profile of $f$ supports a family of at least $\exp(cp)$ real-valued functions with identical Fourier magnitudes and identical Fourier ratio, any two of which are separated by at least $c\sqrt p$ in $\ell^2$. Consequently, every deterministic reconstruction procedure that recovers all members of this family from bounded-precision point evaluations must use at least $c_Bp$ samples, where $c_B>0$ depends only on the number of bits used to encode each observation. The arithmetic input is unconditional and relies only on the Weil bound for mixed character sums, the evaluation of the quadratic Gauss sum, and elementary character identities.

</details>


### [103] [On an inhomogeneous uniform Littlewood type problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08114)
*Johannes Schleischitz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明完全非齐次均匀Littlewood型问题的否定答案，且反例构成稠密Gδ集；将该文作者近期关于齐次情形的类似结果推广到一般情形。主要难点在于半齐次情形（一个线性形式齐次，另一个带无理移位的非齐次），并进一步探讨了高维类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将作者近期关于齐次均匀Littlewood型问题的否定结果推广到完全非齐次情形，并处理半齐次情形这一主要难点，同时探索高维推广。

Method: 通过构造稠密Gδ集的反例集合，证明完全非齐次均匀Littlewood型问题的否定答案；重点攻克半齐次情形（一个线性形式齐次、另一个带无理移位的非齐次）的技术难点。

Result: 完全非齐次均匀Littlewood型问题存在否定答案，反例构成稠密Gδ集；成功处理了半齐次情形；给出了高维类比的结果。

Conclusion: 完全非齐次均匀Littlewood型问题的否定答案成立，且反例具有拓扑大性（稠密Gδ集），该结果是对齐次情形结论的完整推广，并拓展至更高维度。

Abstract: We show that a fully inhomogeneous uniform Littlewood type problem has a negative answer and the counterexamples form a dense $G_δ$ set. This extends the author's recent analogous result for the homogeneous case. The main difficulty in the general setting is the semi-homogeneous case where one linear form is homogeneous and the other inhomogeneous with irrational shift. We further address the higher dimensional analogue.

</details>


### [104] [$L^{\infty}$-norm bounds for Siegel--Jacobi cusp forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08121)
*Anilatmaja Aryasomayajula,Jürg Kramer,Anna-Maria von Pippich*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了任意亏格 g≥1 的 Siegel–Jacobi 尖形式的显式一致 L^∞ 范数上界，这是首次同时给出关于权 k 和指标 m 的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏对任意亏格 Siegel–Jacobi 尖形式的 L^∞ 范数关于权 k 和指标 m 的显式上界估计。

Method: 利用高亏格 Eichler–Zagier theta 分解，将 Siegel–Jacobi 尖形式表示为半整数权 Siegel 尖形式与高维 Jacobi theta 函数的有限线性组合；再基于 Kramer–Mandal 关于 Siegel 尖形式平均 L^∞ 范数的 Bergman 核方法。

Result: 对 k∈Z_{≥g+1}, m∈Z_{≥1} 及任意 ε>0，L^2-归一化的 Siegel–Jacobi 尖形式满足：||φ||_{L^∞} = O_{Γ_0,ε}(k^{(3g^2+5g)/8} m^{g^2+5g/4+ε})。

Conclusion: 首次给出了任意亏格下同时依赖于权 k 和指标 m 的显式 L^∞ 范数上界估计。

Abstract: In this article, we establish explicit and uniform $L^{\infty}$-norm bounds for $L^{2}$-normalized Siegel--Jacobi cusp forms of integral weight $k$ and index $m$ for the Siegel modular group $Γ_{0}=\mathrm{Sp}_{2g}(\mathbb{Z})$ for arbitrary genus $g\geq 1$. Using the generalization of the classical Eichler--Zagier theta decomposition to higher genus, any such Siegel--Jacobi cusp form can be written as a finite linear combination of Siegel cusp forms of half-integral weight $k-1/2$ multiplied by the higher-dimensional analogues of the classical Jacobi theta functions. By building upon the uniform $L^{\infty} $-norm bounds on average for Siegel cusp forms established by J.~Kramer and A.~Mandal~\cite{k1} via the associated Bergman kernels, we prove that for $k\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq g+1}$, $m\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}$, and a given $ε>0$, the $L^{\infty}$-norm bound
  \begin{equation*} \Vertφ\Vert_{L^{\infty}}=\sup_{(τ,z)\in\mathbb{H}_{g}\times\mathbb{C}^{g}}\Vertφ(τ,z)\Vert_{\mathrm{Pet}}=O_ {Γ_{0},ε}\big(k^{(3g^{2}+5g)/8}\,m^{g^{2}+5g/4+ε}\big)
  \end{equation*} holds for any Siegel--Jacobi cusp form $φ$ that is $L^{2}$-normalized with respect to the Petersson inner product. These estimates provide the first explicit upper bounds in terms of both parameters $k$ and $m$ for arbitrary genus $g$.

</details>


### [105] [On a parametric congruence concerning generalized central trinomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08300)
*Chen Wang,Xia-Han Cui*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用常数项方法和生成函数，证明了关于广义中心三项式系数的一个参数化同余式，并由此验证了孙智伟的几个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义中心三项式系数（定义为其常数项）的模同余性质，并验证孙智伟提出的若干相关猜想。

Method: 采用常数项方法（constant term method）和生成函数（generating functions）技术，证明了一个参数化的同余式。

Result: 得到了一个关于广义中心三项式系数的参数化同余式，并作为应用，证实了孙智伟的多个猜想。

Conclusion: 该参数化同余式具有一般性，能有效推导并验证相关猜想。

Abstract: For any $n\in\mathbb{N}=\{0,1,2,\ldots\}$ and $b,c\in\mathbb{C}$, the $n$-th generalized central trinomial coefficient is defined as the constant term in the Laurent expansion of $(b+x+cx^{-1})^n$. In this paper, utilizing the constant term method and generating functions, we prove a parametric congruence concerning generalized central trinomial coefficients. As applications, we confirm several conjectures of Z.-W. Sun.

</details>


### [106] [Explicit estimates of the weighted sum $S(x)=\sum_{n \leq x} (-2)^{Ω(n)} \log\bigl(\frac{x}{n}\bigr).$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08318)
*Riddhi Manna*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了振荡算术函数(-2)^{Ω(n)}的部分和W(x)及其对数加权平均S(x)的界，通过复分析方法（如对数加权Perron公式）证明了|S(x)| ≤ 1.6x，这与Sun猜想|W(x)| < x (x≥3078)相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并推进Sun关于算术函数(-2)^{Ω(n)}部分和W(x)的猜想：对于所有x≥3078，有|W(x)| < x。该猜想涉及具有振荡性质的乘法函数的增长界问题。

Method: 采用复分析方法，特别是对数加权Perron公式（log-weighted Perron's formula），研究对数加权平均S(x)的界，通过计算得到|S(x)| ≤ 1.6x的界。

Result: 成功证明了对数加权平均S(x)满足|S(x)| ≤ 1.6x。

Conclusion: 该结果是对Sun猜想的部分进展，为最终证明原猜想提供了重要工具和界估计。

Abstract: We study the oscillatory arithmetic function $(-2)^{Ω(n)}$, where $Ω(n)$ counts the number of prime factors of $n$, with multiplicity. Sun conjectured a bound on its partial sums $W(x) = \sum_{n \leq x}^{} (-2)^{Ω(n)}$ as $|W(x)| < x$ for all $x \geq 3078$. In this direction, we obtain new bounds for its logarithmically weighted average \begin{equation*}
  S(x)=\sum_{n \leq x} (-2)^{Ω(n)} \log\biggl(\frac{x}{n}\biggr). \end{equation*} Using complex-analytic methods such as the log-weighted Perron's formula, we computed the bound $|S(x)| \leq 1.6x$

</details>


### [107] [Explicit upper and lower bounds on certain functions related to $ζ(s)$ for $s>1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08342)
*Stephan Ramon Garcia,Loïc Grenié,Giuseppe Molteni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为Riemann zeta函数ζ(s)及其导数在s>1区域提供了显式的上下界，改进了现有关于(s-1)ζ(s)及其导数、以及(ζ'/ζ)(s)的界限估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Riemann zeta函数及其导数在s>1区域的界限估计不够精确，需要更紧的显式上下界来支持数论中的相关应用。

Method: 通过解析方法推导显式不等式，对(s-1)ζ(s)及其导数、以及ζ'/ζ(s)等常用表达式给出精确的上下界。

Result: 得到了比已有结果更优的显式上界和下界，改进了相关表达式的估计精度。

Conclusion: 本文提供的新界限可应用于数论中需要zeta函数精确估计的场景，是对现有理论的重要改进。

Abstract: We provide explicit upper and lower bounds for certain commonly occurring expressions that involve the Riemann zeta function $ζ(s)$ and its derivatives on $s>1$. For example, this improves upon existing bounds for $(s-1)ζ(s)$ and its derivatives, and for $(ζ'/ζ)(s)$.

</details>


### [108] [Proof of a conjecture of Andrews and El Bachraoui on the parity of two-color partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08369)
*Koustav Banerjee,Kathrin Bringmann*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Andrews和El Bachraoui关于某种双色分拆奇偶性的猜想，即若某类Fourier系数t_o(n)为奇数，则8n+9可表示为x²+2y²形式的二元二次型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Andrews和El Bachraoui提出的关于双色分拆奇偶性的猜想，深入理解分拆函数与二次型表示之间的深层数论联系。

Method: 通过分析相应q级数的Fourier系数t_o(n)的奇偶性，建立其与二元二次型x²+2y²表示整数8n+9之间的等价关系，运用模形式或q级数恒等式等工具完成证明。

Result: 成功证明了若t_o(n)为奇数，则8n+9必然可表示为x²+2y²的形式，从而证实了Andrews和El Bachraoui的猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了特定双色分拆函数的奇偶性与二次型表示之间的深刻联系，为分拆理论中奇偶性问题的研究提供了新的见解。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove a conjecture of Andrews and El Bachraoui concerning the parity of certain two-color partitions. Precisely, we show that if the Fourier coefficient $t_o(n)$ of the corresponding $q$-series is odd, then $8n+9$ is represented by the binary quadratic form $x^2+2y^2$.

</details>


### [109] [Rademacher-type formula and higher order Turán inequalities for $\ell$-regular overpartitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08460)
*Priyanka Dey,Ajit Singh,Gurinder Singh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对平方自由奇整数ℓ，利用圆法推导了无ℓ倍数分部的过剩分割数$\overline{A}_\ell(n)$的Rademacher型公式，并应用Griffin等人结果证明了ℓ-正规过剩分割函数的高阶Turán不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平方自由奇整数ℓ条件下，无ℓ倍数分部的过剩分割数的精确渐近公式及其分析性质。

Method: 采用圆法（circle method）推导Rademacher型精确公式。

Result: 得到了$\overline{A}_\ell(n)$的Rademacher型公式，并推导出高阶Turán不等式。

Conclusion: 成功扩展了过剩分割函数在特定模数下的分析性质，建立了其高阶Turán不等式的有效性。

Abstract: For $\ell\geq 2$, let $\overline{A}_\ell(n)$ count the number of overpartitions of $n$ with no parts divisible by $\ell$. In this article, we employ the circle method to derive a Rademacher-type formula for $\overline{A}_\ell(n)$, when $\ell$ is a squarefree odd integer. As an application, we derive higher order Tuŕan inequalities for the $\ell$-regular overpartition function using a result of Griffin, Ono, Rolen, and Zagier.

</details>


### [110] [Modular Nahm Sums for the Inverse Cartan Matrix of Type $D_r$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08606)
*Liuquan Wang,Shangwen Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对D_r型Cartan矩阵的逆矩阵，部分证明了Sun-Wang关于Nahm和模性及Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式的猜想，构造了⌊(r+4)/2⌋个模Nahm和，并通过Bailey对方法建立了相应的恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sun和Wang猜想对于D_r型Cartan矩阵的逆矩阵及零向量存在一个Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式，并进一步猜想存在r-1个伴随模Nahm和与非零向量相关。本文旨在部分证明该猜想。

Method: 利用Bailey对方法建立多种Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式，构造⌊(r+4)/2⌋个模Nahm和，并证明其模性。

Result: 部分证明了Sun-Wang猜想，构造了⌊(r+4)/2⌋个与𝒞(D_r)^{-1}相关的模Nahm和，并证实了猜想中的恒等式。

Conclusion: 通过Bailey对方法成功建立了若干Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式，部分验证了Sun-Wang关于D_r型Cartan矩阵逆矩阵的Nahm和模性猜想。

Abstract: For $r\geq 3$ we denote by $\mathcal{C}(D_r)$ the Cartan matrix of type $D_r$. Recently, Sun and Wang conjectured a Rogers--Ramanujan type identity for the Nahm sum associated with $\mathcal{C}(D_r)^{-1}$ and the zero vector. They further conjecture that there exist $r-1$ companion modular Nahm sums associated with nonzero vectors. We partially prove this conjecture by constructing $\lfloor (r+4)/2\rfloor$ modular Nahm sums for $\mathcal{C}(D_r)^{-1}$. To prove their modularity, we utilize the method of Bailey pairs to establish various Rogers--Ramanujan type identities. In particular, we confirm their conjectural identity.

</details>


### [111] [Reduction modulo p of crystalline Galois representations via μ_p-equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08660)
*Bhargav Bhatt,Toby Gee,Mark Kisin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Q_p的绝对Galois群的晶体表示，在给定Hodge-Tate权重的条件下，得到了其模p约化的惯性权重的新约束，并由此提出了适用于Q_p上非分歧连通约化群的L-参数的显式Serre权重猜想，且证明了该猜想的消去方向。证明利用棱镜技术展示了附属于晶体Galois表示的Breuil-Kisin模的模p约化具有自然的μ_p-等变结构，并结合仿射Grassmannian的μ_p-不动点的几何结果导出了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Q_p的绝对Galois群的晶体表示，获得其模p约化惯性权重的新约束，并推广Serre权重猜想到更一般的L-参数情形。

Method: 利用棱镜（prismatic）技术证明Breuil-Kisin模的模p约化具有μ_p-等变结构，再结合仿射Grassmannian的μ_p-不动点几何结果导出约束。

Result: 得到了晶体表示模p约化的惯性权重的新约束，提出了显式Serre权重猜想，并证明了消去方向。

Conclusion: 在Q_p上非分歧连通约化群的L-参数一般性下，建立了Serre权重猜想的消去方向。

Abstract: For a crystalline representation of the absolute Galois group of Q_p, with given Hodge-Tate weights, we obtain new constraints on the inertial weights of its mod p reduction. This allows us to formulate an explicit Serre weight conjecture, in the generality of L-parameters for unramified connected reductive groups over Q_p, and to prove the elimination direction of this conjecture. The proof uses prismatic techniques to show that the reductions modulo p of the Breuil-Kisin modules attached to crystalline Galois representations acquire a natural μ_p-equivariant structure. Combining this with results on the geometry of the μ_p-fixed points of affine Grassmannians leads to our new constraint.

</details>


### [112] [A Matrix Analogue of Rational Number Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08732)
*Anjelo Gabriel R. Cruz,Manuel Joseph C. Loquias*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究在交换整数矩阵对(P,Q)下，通过有限数字集构造具有有限性和唯一性性质的P/Q展开系统，并在二维情形下利用有限自动机构建同时满足两种性质的数字系统，同时提出通过扩展树获取实数向量展开的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字展开系统理论中，将整数向量表示为基底的幂级数形式是数论和动力系统的重要问题。本文旨在寻找合适的数字集，使得给定的矩阵对(P,Q)能够生成具有有限性和唯一性性质的展开系统，从而推广经典数字系统到高维矩阵情形。

Method: 1. 定义P/Q展开系统及其有限性和唯一性性质；2. 推导数字集存在的充分条件；3. 针对二维情形(d=2)，利用有限自动机方法构造同时满足两种性质的数字系统；4. 引入扩展树(expansion tree)概念，将整数向量的展开推广到实数向量。

Result: 给出了数字集存在的充分条件；在二维情形下成功构造了兼具有限性和唯一性性质的数字系统；提出了通过扩展树获取实数向量P/Q展开的方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立了矩阵数字展开系统的一般框架，为高维数字系统理论提供了新的构造工具，特别是有限自动机方法在二维情形下有效解决了同时满足有限性和唯一性性质的数字集构造问题。

Abstract: Let $P,Q \in \mathbb{Z}^{d\times d}$ be invertible coprime matrices such that all the eigenvalues of $Q^{-1}P$ have modulus greater than 1, and $\mathbb{Z}^d[Q^{-1}P]$ be the smallest non-trivial $Q^{-1}P$-invariant $\mathbb{Z}$-module containing $\mathbb{Z}^d$. Suppose there is a finite digit set $\mathcal{D}\subseteq \mathbb{Z}^d + P\mathbb{Z}^d[Q^{-1}P]$ for which every vector $x \in \mathbb{Z}^d + P\mathbb{Z}^d[Q^{-1}P]$ can be represented in the form
  \[
  x = \sum_{i=0}^{\ell-1} (Q^{-1}P)^i Q^{-1}d_i,
  \]
  where the digits $d_i \in \mathcal{D}$ for all $i \in \{0,1,\ldots,\ell-1\}$. We call such a representation a $P/Q$-expansion of $x$, and we say that the digit system $(P,Q,\mathcal{D})$ has the finiteness property. If, in addition, $\mathcal{D}$ is a complete set of residues of the quotient group $(\mathbb{Z}^d + P\mathbb{Z}^d[Q^{-1}P])/P\mathbb{Z}^d[Q^{-1}P]$, then the digits $d_0, d_1, \dots, d_{\ell-1}$ in the $P/Q$-expansion of $x$ are unique whenever $\ell \in \mathbb{Z}^+$ is minimal, and the resulting digit system is said to have the uniqueness property. We present sufficient conditions for the existence of a digit set $\mathcal{D}$ in which $(P,Q,\mathcal{D})$ has the finiteness property. For $d=2$, we make use of finite automata to construct digit systems $(P,Q,\mathcal{D})$ having both the finiteness and uniqueness properties. We also obtain the $P/Q$-expansion of a vector $x$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ by means of the so-called expansion tree of the digit system $(P,Q,\mathcal{D})$.

</details>
